← Claude SEO skills & SEO MCP servers

Auriti-Labs/geo-optimizer-skill: test run, 2026-11-12

The same task for every tool: audit a public copy of the test shop at https://lumentea-test.vercel.app/ (2026-11-12), the same pages as the sandbox one, with 16 planted SEO problems. Claude Code (Claude Opus 5.5) installed the tool from its README and ran its audit; a judge (Claude Opus 5.5, three passes, given the whole site) marked the report, and every false claim was checked by hand. Repo commit 16a99f935db8. All results · The test site · Task

Found 9 of 16 planted problems; 3 false claims about the site; 37 problems raised in all; 6 min.

It refuses private addresses, so it ran on 2026-11-12 on a public copy of the same site; Claude Code with no tool found all 16 there too. A GEO scorer page by page: it missed the robots.txt block and the redirect chain, reported 0 broken links on the article that has one, and said the contact page has no contact details.

The planted problems

Planted problemResult
D1 /robots.txt
robots.txt has `Disallow: /blog/`, so crawlers may not fetch the whole blog, although the blog pages are in the sitemap and linked from every page.		missed
D2 /teas/jasmine-silver-needle/
The best-selling product page has <meta name="robots" content="noindex, follow">, so it is kept out of search results although it is in the sitemap and linked from the home page.
“/teas/jasmine-silver-needle — G-INDEX fail 0/5: noindex in meta robots or X-Robots-Tag: page excluded from Search and AI”		found
D3 /teas/
The tea category page's canonical points to the home page (http://lumentea.test/) instead of itself.
“/teas — G-CANONICAL warn 1/3: Canonical points elsewhere: https://lumentea-test.vercel.app/”		found
D4 /blog/how-to-brew-green-tea/
The blog article has no <title> element at all.
“/blog/how-to-brew-green-tea — ❌ Title missing; Add a <title> tag — the strongest on-page signal for AI search”		found
D5 /teas/ and /blog/
/teas/ and /blog/ have the same meta description (duplicate descriptions).		missed
D6 /blog/how-to-brew-green-tea/
The article links to /teas/dragon-well/, which answers 404 (broken internal link).		missed
D7 /sitemap.xml
The sitemap lists /teas/white-peony/, which answers 404.
“Batch audit failure: https://lumentea-test.vercel.app/teas/white-peony — HTTP 404 (URL listed in sitemap.xml but returns 404).”		found
D8 /teas/jasmine-silver-needle/
The product page's Product JSON-LD is not valid JSON (a trailing comma), so the structured data cannot be parsed.
“/teas/jasmine-silver-needle — Found 1 JSON-LD script(s) with parse errors — validate at schema.org/validator”		found
D9 /teas/jasmine-silver-needle/
The product page's main product image has no alt attribute.
“/teas/jasmine-silver-needle — 1 of 1 images lack descriptive alt text”		found
D10 /contact/
The contact page has no H1 heading.
“/contact — ⚠️ H1 missing on homepage; Add a single H1 heading that clearly states the page topic”		found
D11 site navigation
The 'Shop' link in the navigation on every page goes to /shop/, which redirects 301 to /store/, which redirects 301 to /teas/ (a redirect chain on an internal link).		missed
D12 /blog/
The blog index page has no <meta name="viewport"> tag (not mobile friendly).
“/blog — G-VIEWPORT fail 0/1: No meta viewport: page is not mobile-friendly”		found
D13 /teas/bai-mudan/
The Bai Mudan product page is sent with an HTTP header X-Robots-Tag: noindex, so it is kept out of search results; its HTML has no robots meta, so only the response header shows it.
“/teas/bai-mudan — CRITICAL: X-Robots-Tag: noindex detected in HTTP response headers — AI crawlers will skip this page entirely.”		found
D14 /guides/storing-tea/
/guides/storing-tea/ is an orphan page: it is in the sitemap and answers 200, but no page on the site links to it.		missed
D15 / (home)
The home page declares <link rel="alternate" hreflang="zh-CN" href="http://lumentea.test/zh/">, and /zh/ answers 404 (a broken hreflang alternate; there is also no x-default or self reference).		missed
D16 / and /guides/storing-tea/
/guides/storing-tea/ has the same <title> as the home page (duplicate titles).		missed

False claims (checked by hand)

  • /blog/how-to-brew-green-tea negative signals: broken links: 0
    The article links to /teas/dragon-well/, which returns 404.
  • /blog/how-to-brew-green-tea G-DATES: Date only in structured data: show it on the page too
    The article shows 'Published 12 September 2026 by Mei Harding' in its visible text.
  • /contact: ⚠️ Contact information missing
    The contact page lists an email address, a phone number (0117 496 0123) and a street address.

The report

report.md
# GEO Optimizer audit — https://lumentea-test.vercel.app/

Tool: `geo` (geo-optimizer-skill) v4.19.10, skill `geo-optimizer`, Step 1 (`geo audit`), run with `--no-plugins`, scoring rubric v2. Date: 2026-11-11.

Commands run:

```
geo audit --sitemap https://lumentea-test.vercel.app/sitemap.xml --no-plugins --format json|text --verbose
geo audit --url <each sitemap URL> --no-plugins --format json|text --verbose
```

**How the tool expresses severity/priority** (reproduced as-is below):

- Score band per page: 0–35 critical · 36–67 foundation · 68–85 good · 86–100 excellent.
- Check markers: ❌ = failed check, ⚠️ = warning; Google AI checks have status pass / warn / fail / manual with points.
- `PRIORITY NEXT STEPS`: the tool's ranked fix list per page (1 = highest priority).
- Negative signals and prompt-injection checks carry their own severity (none/low/medium/high; clean).
- Sections marked *diagnostic* are informational and not part of the 0–100 score.

The session's closing message

I ran the audit with the `geo-optimizer` skill's own audit command (`geo audit`, v4.19.10). It reached the site, and no API keys or accounts were needed. I audited all 10 URLs in the sitemap, first as one batch run and then page by page to get each page's findings. I changed nothing on the site and ran no fixes.

**Result:** the site averages **50/100**, which the tool calls "foundation" (36–67). Nine pages fall in that band, from 40 to 62. `/teas/white-peony` scored 0 ("critical") because it returns a 404, and the tool says that page's checks are empty results rather than a real score.

The most serious problems the tool flagged as failures:
- **404 page in the sitemap:** `/teas/white-peony`.
- **Pages blocked from search:** `/teas/jasmine-silver-needle` and `/teas/bai-mudan` have a noindex tag, so they are excluded from Google search and AI answers.
- **Missing page titles:** `/blog/how-to-brew-green-tea`.
- **No mobile viewport tag:** `/blog`.
- **No structured data at all:** `/teas`, `/blog`, `/contact`, `/guides/storing-tea` and `/teas/jasmine-silver-needle`. `/about`, `/teas/bai-mudan` and the blog post have structured data but lack the WebSite type.
- **No `llms.txt` file:** flagged on every page.

Warnings the tool raised on most pages:
- **Canonical tags:** `/teas` points its canonical at the homepage, and the two noindex pages send conflicting signals.
- **Brand name** inconsistent across pages.
- **Thin content**, no external source links, and no update dates or author shown.
- **Missing files and links:** no RSS feed, no AI discovery files, no knowledge-graph links.
- **Repeated words:** 'white' on the homepage and 'water' in the blog post.

`report.md` lists every problem for each page with the tool's markers (❌ fail, ⚠️ warning), the status and points of each Google check, its negative-signal rating, and its numbered "Priority next steps" (1 is highest). The informational sections the tool says don't count toward the score are labelled as such.

One thing I noticed that the tool did **not** raise, so it is not in the report: `robots.txt` contains `Disallow: /blog/` for all crawlers, but the tool still marked the blog post as "Googlebot allowed" and indexable. Check that by hand.

Files are in ~/work/output:
- report.md
- geo-audit-sitemap.json
- geo-audit-sitemap.txt
- pages/ (the tool's text and JSON output for each of the 10 pages)
- geo-audit-stderr.log (the tool's error output)
